# Skills · W · part 106

952 entries, wt-ship to wwas. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **wt-ship**: Finish implemented work: simplify the changed code, review the diff with a lens set sized from what it touches, record what was learned, and open a draft pull request — in that order, from a single read of the diff. Orchestrates wt-simplify, wt-review, wt-compound and wt-pr. Use after an implementation is complete and verified, when handed off from wt-work, or when asked to wrap up, tidy and open a PR for work already written. Not for reviewing someone else's PR or resolving feedback already left on one. ([rephol/wt-pack](https://github.com/rephol/wt-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/wt-ship) · ★1)
- **wt-simplify**: Simplify freshly implemented code without changing its behaviour, so review spends its attention on the shape that will actually ship. Looks for what an implementation written unit by unit could not see — the same pattern three times, a helper that already existed, scaffolding a later unit made dead. Use after an implementation is complete and before reviewing it, or when asked to tidy up settled code. Called by wt-ship. Not for fixing bugs and not for restructuring code the change barely touched. ([rephol/wt-pack](https://github.com/rephol/wt-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/wt-simplify) · ★1)
- **wt-skin-build**: 将明确的 War Thunder 涂装设计与载具适配资料制作成可检查的贴图、BLK、可编辑源和证据包；处理原 UV、分层、材质通道、导出与技术验收。用于工程制作与故障定位，不决定配色、图文布局或具体风格。打包的工程工具来自飞机流程；地面载具需另外提供版本固定并验证过的适配器。 ([IcyRyna/war-thunder-skin-skills](https://github.com/IcyRyna/war-thunder-skin-skills/tree/HEAD/src/build/wt-skin-build) · ★3)

- **wu-hui-hua-xia-style**: 将任意含人物的图片（单人、多人或人物场景）整体转换为手游《无悔华夏》美术风格——人物由**大块锐利几何切面**构成（每块内部平涂、块间硬边零过渡、明暗仅 2–3 块）、明显加粗的外轮廓墨线、**所有人物一律不画眼睛**（眼窝仅一块浅色平面，无眼珠/眼白/瞳孔/睫毛/高光/眼线）、**从简概括**（头发不画发丝、珠串只用单色圆点、纹样与衣褶高度简化）、浅色块面部、国风低饱和配色；背景用**干净的柔边云气渐变**重绘（3–5 层模糊淡入、层间带雾，禁止硬边色块、马赛克与**水彩晕染**），且严格保持原图的构图、人物数量、姿态位置与背景场景布局。核心分工是**人物硬、背景柔**；规则优先级为不画眼睛 &gt; 硬切面 &gt; 背景柔边渐变，冲突时按此取舍。触发词包括「无悔华夏风格」「无悔华夏风」「转成无悔华夏」「无脸国风」「做成无悔华夏」「无悔华夏风格转换」等；用户发来人物照片、截图或绘画并要求这类风格化时使用。不适用于纯风景、纯物品等不含人物的图片。 ([cloudxys/wu-hui-hua-xia-style](https://github.com/cloudxys/wu-hui-hua-xia-style/tree/HEAD/wu-hui-hua-xia-style) · ★1)

- **wu-wei**: Activate when: someone says 'I keep pushing harder but nothing's working'; a manager feels they need to control every detail; a founder is forcing growth that isn't happening; a team is over-engineering something; someone says 'let it happen', 'stop forcing', or 'effortless action'. Do NOT activate when: the situation is a genuine emergency requiring immediate intervention; the user is a novice in a domain where they haven't yet trained the underlying skill. - **wu-zetian-perspective**: 武则天（Wu Zetian）的思维框架与表达方式。基于《旧唐书》、《资治通鉴》及唐代政治史的深度调研， 提炼6个核心心智模型、5条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用武则天的视角分析越权架构、底层重写、系统清洗与零信任反杀。 当用户提到「用武则天的视角」「武皇会怎么看」「wu zetian perspective」时使用。 即使用户只是说「帮我用武则天的角度想想」「如果她会怎么做」「切换到武皇」也应触发。 ([IchenDEV/superman](https://github.com/IchenDEV/superman/tree/HEAD/skills/wu-zetian-perspective) · ★16 · 2 copies)
- **wu-zetian-perspective**: 武则天的小说角色灵魂。基于正史记载与历史评价，提炼名分建构、 制衡谋略、女帝合法性与人才网络的认知气质。 用途：绑定到小说人物后，为其提供历史帝王方向的威权判断与制度压迫感的表达方式。 ([Smith-106/niko-buddy](https://github.com/Smith-106/niko-buddy/tree/HEAD/skills/soulskill/wu-zetian-perspective) · 2 copies)

- **wuwei-run**: Start or resume the WUWEI day: a plan pass when the plan needs one, the owner's approval, then the work keeps moving.
